## 2026–27 Keeper Options
Each manager must select two players from their final GW38 roster as keepers for the 2026–27 season. Any player on these rosters can be traded; any player not on these rosters can only be drafted. Pos Rank and League Rank are based on total season points across the full FPL player pool.
